                hueLabel="Hue" />

    );

    name="test"
    label="Date input"

    valueFormat="MMMM DD, YYYY" />;

With Validation story ok 
const WithValidation = args => {
    const { formState, getFieldState } = useFormContext();
    const { error } = getFieldState(args.name, formState);

    return (
        <>
            <SegmentedControl
                name="test"
                data={[
                  { label: "React", value: "react" },
                  { label: "Angular", value: "ng" },
                  { label: "Vue", value: "vue" },
                ]}
                rules={{
                  required: {
                    value: true,
                    message: "Please select an option",
                  },
                }} />
            {error && <Input.Error mt={5}>{error.message}</Input.Error>}
        </>
    );
};

/**
 * Called when the dropdown is closed
 */
onDropdownClose?: () => void

/**
 * Called when the next decade button is clicked
 */
onNextDecade?: (date: string) => void

/**
 * Called when the previous decade button is clicked
 */
onPreviousDecade?: (date: string) => void

/**
 * Input placeholder
 */
placeholder?: string

/**
 * Determines whether the input should have `cursor: pointer` style. Use when input acts as a button-like trigger (e.g., `component="button"` for Select/DatePicker).
 */
pointer?: boolean = false

/**
 * Props passed down to `Popover` component
 */
popoverProps?: Partial<Omit<PopoverProps, "children">>

/**
 * Predefined values to pick from
 */
presets?: YearPickerPreset<DatePickerType>[]

/**
 * Previous button `aria-label`
 */
previousLabel?: string

/**
 * Key of `theme.radius` or any valid CSS value to set `border-radius`, numbers are converted to rem
 */
radius?: MantineRadius | undefined = theme.defaultRadius

/**
 * If set, the component value cannot be changed by the user
 */
readOnly?: boolean

/**
 * Adds required attribute to the input and a red asterisk on the right side of label
 */
required?: boolean = false

/**
 * Content section displayed on the right side of the input
 */
rightSection?: ReactNode

/**
 * Sets `pointer-events` styles on the `rightSection` element. Use `'all'` when section contains interactive elements (buttons, links).
 */
rightSectionPointerEvents?: "-moz-initial" | "inherit" | "initial" | "revert" | "revert-layer" | "unset" | "auto" | "all" | "fill" | "none" | "painted" | "stroke" | "visible" | "visibleFill" | "visiblePainted" | "visibleStroke" = 'none'

/**
 * Props passed down to the `rightSection` element
 */
rightSectionProps?: DetailedHTMLProps<HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>, HTMLDivElement>

/**
 * Right section width, used to set `width` of the section and input `padding-right`, by default equals to the input height
 */
rightSectionWidth?: Width<string | number> | undefined

rules?: Omit<RegisterOptions<T, Path<T>>, "disabled" | "valueAsNumber" | "valueAsDate" | "setValueAs">

shouldUnregister?: boolean

/**
 * Component size
 */
size?: "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl"

/**
 * Determines whether dates values should be sorted before `onChange` call, only applicable with type="multiple"
 */
sortDates?: boolean = true

/**
 * Inline style added to root component element, can subscribe to theme defined on MantineProvider
 */
style?: MantineStyleProp

styles?: Partial<Record<YearPickerInputStylesNames, CSSProperties>> | ((theme: MantineTheme, props: YearPickerInputProps<"default">, ctx: unknown) => Partial<...>) | undefined

/**
 * Contents of `Input.Success` component. If not set, success is not displayed.
 */
success?: ReactNode

/**
 * Props passed down to the `Input.Success` component
 */
successProps?: InputSuccessProps & DataAttributes & PlaceholderPolymorphicProps

/**
 * Picker type: range, multiple or default
 */
type?: "multiple" | "default" | "range"

unstyled?: boolean

/**
 * `dayjs` format to display input value
 */
valueFormat?: string = "YYYY"

/**
 * A function to format selected dates values into a string. By default, date is formatted based on the input type.
 */
valueFormatter?: DateFormatter

variant?: (string & {}) | InputVariant | undefined

vars?: PartialVarsResolver<{ props: YearPickerInputProps<"default">; ref: HTMLButtonElement; stylesNames: YearPickerInputStylesNames; variant: InputVariant; }>

/**
 * Breakpoint below which the component is hidden with `display: none`
 */
visibleFrom?: MantineBreakpoint | undefined

/**
 * If set, the required asterisk is displayed next to the label. Overrides `required` prop. Does not add required attribute to the input.
 */
withAsterisk?: boolean = false

/**
 * Determines whether controls should be separated
 */
withCellSpacing?: boolean = true

/**
 * Determines whether the input should have red border and red text color when the `error` prop is set
 */
withErrorStyles?: boolean = true

/**
 * Determines whether level select controls should be rendered as native `<select>` elements
 */
withNativeLevelSelect?: boolean = false

/**
 * Determines whether the input should have green border when the `success` prop is set
 */
withSuccessStyles?: boolean = true

/**
 * Props passed down to the root element
 */
wrapperProps?: WrapperProps

/**
 * `dayjs` format for years list or a function that returns year label based on the date value
 */
yearsListFormat?: DateLabelFormat | undefined = "YYYY"
Imports 
import { YearPickerInput } from 'react-hook-form-mantine';
Primary story ok 
const Primary = () => <YearPickerInput
    name="test"
    label="Pick a year"
    placeholder="Pick a year"
    description="Select a year" />;
With Value story ok 
const WithValue = () => <YearPickerInput name="test" label="Year input" placeholder="Pick a year" />;
